            ):
                raise ProtocolError("replay_plan_semantics_mismatch")
            for step, expected in zip(body["steps"], expected_steps):
                ReplayValidator._validate_replay_step(step, expected)
        for payload in envelopes:
            if payload["message_type"] != "result":
                continue
            body = payload["body"]
            decision = decisions.get(payload["request_id"])
            if decision is None:
                raise ProtocolError("replay_result_without_decision")
            matching = [
                step for step in decision["body"]["steps"] if step["action_id"] == body["action_id"]
            ]
            if payload["state_ref"] != body["state_ref"]:
                raise ProtocolError("replay_result_contradiction")
            if len(matching) != 1 or matching[0]["operation"] != body["operation"]:
                raise ProtocolError("replay_result_binding_mismatch")
            if body["engine_revision"] != body["state_ref"]["revision"]:
                raise ProtocolError("replay_result_contradiction")
            transition_error = _result_transition_error(
                body["operation"], body["status"], decision["state_ref"], body["state_ref"]
            )
            if transition_error == "stale_result":
                raise ProtocolError("replay_stale_result")
            if transition_error is not None:
                raise ProtocolError("replay_result_transition_mismatch")
        return {"valid": True, "rows": len(envelopes), "content_hashes": hashes}


def default_envelope(
    *,
    message_type: str,
    body: Mapping[str, Any],
    session_id: str = "session-1",
    session_epoch: int = 0,
    request_id: str = "request-1",
    sequence: int = 0,
    delivery_index: int = 0,
    state_ref: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    policy_hash: str = "0" * 64,
    arm: str = "P",
    backend: str = "codex",
    model: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    source = {
        "session_id": session_id,
        "session_epoch": session_epoch,
        "episode_id": "episode-1",
        "request_id": request_id,
        "sequence": sequence,
        "delivery_index": delivery_index,
        "config_hash": canonical_config_hash(),
        "policy_hash": policy_hash,
        "state_ref": dict(state_ref or {"revision": 0, "sha256": "0" * 64}),
        "budget": dict(DEFAULT_BUDGET),
        "provenance": {
            "arm": arm,
            "backend": backend,
            "model": model,
            "trace_ref": None,
            "recorded_response_ref": None,
            "raw_utterance_sha256": None,
        },
    }
    if message_type == "request" and body.get("event_kind") == "utterance":
        utterance = body.get("utterance")
        if not isinstance(utterance, str) or not utterance:
            raise ProtocolError("invalid_utterance")
        source["provenance"]["raw_utterance_sha256"] = hashlib.sha256(
            utterance.encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
    return GoalController.make_envelope(source, message_type, body)

    "BudgetExceeded",
    "ControllerFailure",
    "GoalController",
    "IntentInterpreter",
    "PlanningContext",
    "ProtocolError",
    "ReplayValidator",
    "canonical_config_hash",
    "canonical_json",
    "content_hash",
    "default_envelope",
    "sha256_json",
    "validate_envelope",
    "validate_observation",
    "verify_claims",
]

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Generate additive bilingual NPC evidence fragments and an editable SVG figure.

This generator consumes only the new NPC-GOAL-RECOVERY packet. Legacy E1/E2/E3/ENG1
fragments are deliberately not read or rewritten here.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from collections import Counter
from html import escape
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PACKET = ROOT / "research/academic-pipeline/npc-goal-recovery/verified-run-003"
CONFIG = ROOT / "configs/npc-goal-recovery.json"
GAME_CONFIG = ROOT / "game-track/godot/project.godot"
SCHEMA = ROOT / "game-track/schemas/npc-goal-recovery.schema.json"
OUT = ROOT / "paper/latex/generated"
FIGURES = ROOT / "paper/latex/figures"
PROJECT_CONFIG_SHA256 = "2e7966bf2ac6b54bfaf2db84b3f446686436bd3a1f8efea592ffa82e91249edd"
NPC_CONFIG_SHA256 = "d63f5679f360d3fd8d1a424422f5cae4a7096ba46441f746cdd97de56044e098"
ARMS = ("P", "R", "O")


def _read_json(path: Path) -> Any:
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def _sha(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def _rows(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    rows = []
    for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        if line.strip():
            value = json.loads(line)
            if not isinstance(value, dict):
                raise ValueError(f"{path}: JSONL row is not an object")
            rows.append(value)
    return rows


def _require(condition: bool, message: str) -> None:
    if not condition:
        raise ValueError(message)


def _portable_validation_report(report: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Copy a validation report and make an exact packet path portable."""
    portable = dict(report)
    recorded_dir = portable.get("packet_dir")
    if not isinstance(recorded_dir, str):
        return portable
    recorded_path = Path(recorded_dir)
    if not recorded_path.is_absolute():
        recorded_path = ROOT / recorded_path
    if recorded_path.resolve() == PACKET.resolve():
        portable["packet_dir"] = PACKET.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
    return portable


def _load_packet() -> dict[str, Any]:
    _require(PACKET.is_dir(), f"missing NPC packet: {PACKET}")
    from validate_npc_goal_evidence import validate_packet

    validation_reports: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for packet_id in ("NPC-ENG1", "NPC-SCR1"):
        report, errors = validate_packet(packet_id, ROOT, packet_dir=PACKET)
        _require(not errors and report.get("valid") is True, f"{packet_id}: " + "; ".join(errors))
        validation_reports[packet_id] = _portable_validation_report(report)
    config = _read_json(CONFIG)
    _require(_sha(GAME_CONFIG) == PROJECT_CONFIG_SHA256, "NPC game config SHA file drift")
    _require(_sha(CONFIG) == NPC_CONFIG_SHA256, "NPC protocol config SHA file drift")
    _require(
        config.get("goal", {}).get("id") == "harbor-signal-restored-authorized-tide-lead-v1",
        "NPC goal identity drift",
    )
    success = str(config.get("goal", {}).get("success", ""))
    for predicate in ("signal_lens_installed", "lighthouse_hint_authorized", "tide_marks_hint"):
        _require(predicate in success, f"NPC final predicate omits {predicate}")
    _require(
        config.get("evaluation", {}).get("independent_source_fraction") == 0,
        "NPC O independence drift",
    )
    _require(
        config.get("evaluation", {}).get("utterance_source_kind") == "same_model_family_auxiliary",
        "NPC O source-kind drift",
    )
    aggregate = _read_json(PACKET / "aggregate.json")
    manifest = _read_json(PACKET / "screening-assignment-manifest.json")
    engineering = _rows(PACKET / "engineering-results.jsonl")
    screening = _rows(PACKET / "screening-results.jsonl")
    replay = _read_json(PACKET / "replay-results.json")
    receipts = _read_json(PACKET / "sha256-manifest.json")
    _require(
        aggregate.get("schema_version") == "npc-goal-recovery/evidence-1",
        "NPC aggregate schema drift",
    )
    _require(aggregate.get("packet_id") == "NPC-SCR1", "NPC packet identity drift")
    _require(
        aggregate.get("source_project_config_sha256") == PROJECT_CONFIG_SHA256,
        "NPC project config SHA drift",
    )
    _require(
        aggregate.get("npc_protocol_config_sha256") == NPC_CONFIG_SHA256,
        "NPC protocol config SHA drift",
    )
    _require(aggregate.get("source_schema_sha256") == _sha(SCHEMA), "NPC schema SHA drift")
    _require(len(engineering) == 16, "NPC-ENG1 JSONL cardinality drift")
    _require(len(screening) == 72, "NPC-SCR1 JSONL cardinality drift")
    _require(aggregate.get("run_mode") == "publish", "NPC preflight is not publishable")
    p_rows = [row for row in screening if row.get("arm") == "P"]
    _require(len(p_rows) == 24, "NPC actual P denominator drift")
    model_rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for index, row in enumerate(p_rows, start=1):
        model = row.get("model")
        _require(
            isinstance(model, dict),
            f"NPC P model metadata missing/malformed at row {index}",
        )
        _require(
            "requested" in model and "returned" in model,
            f"NPC P model metadata fields missing at row {index}",
        )
        if "available" in model:
            _require(
                isinstance(model["available"], bool),
                f"NPC P model availability malformed at row {index}",
            )
        for field in ("requested", "returned"):
            value = model[field]
            _require(
                value is None
                or (isinstance(value, str) and bool(value.strip()) and value == value.strip()),
                f"NPC P model {field} malformed at row {index}",
            )
        model_rows.append({"requested": model["requested"], "returned": model["returned"]})
    requested_models = {model["requested"] for model in model_rows}
    returned_models = {model["returned"] for model in model_rows}
    returned_named_models = {model for model in returned_models if model is not None}
    _require(len(requested_models) == 1, "NPC P requested model cohort is mixed")
    _require(
        len(returned_named_models) <= 1,
        "NPC P returned model cohort has multiple named revisions",
    )
    model_metadata = {
        "requested": next(iter(requested_models)),
        "returned": next(iter(returned_named_models), None),
        "returned_unknown_count": sum(model["returned"] is None for model in model_rows),
        "cohort_size": len(model_rows),
    }
    stale_control_counts: Counter[str] = Counter()
    stale_case_controls: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
    stale_arm_counts: Counter[str] = Counter()
    stale_verified_rows = 0
    assigned_failure_rows = 0
    for row in screening:
        if row.get("status") != "assigned_failure":
            continue
        assigned_failure_rows += 1
        failure_codes = {
            failure.get("code") for failure in row.get("failure", []) if isinstance(failure, dict)
        }
        acks = row.get("acks")
        ack_body = acks[0].get("body") if isinstance(acks, list) and acks else None
        trace = row.get("engine", {}).get("experiment", {}).get("trace", {})
        gates = trace.get("expected_negative_gates", []) if isinstance(trace, dict) else []
        controls = {
            gate.get("control")
            for gate in gates
            if isinstance(gate, dict)
            and gate.get("control") in {"stale_revision", "stale_policy"}
            and gate.get("expected_status") == "STALE"
            and gate.get("observed_status") == "STALE"
            and gate.get("state_ref_before") == gate.get("state_ref_after")
            and gate.get("passed") is True
        }
        case_id = row.get("case_id")
        fixture_ref = row.get("fixture_ref")
        verified_stale = (
            "result_ack_not_accepted" in failure_codes
            and isinstance(ack_body, dict)
            and ack_body.get("status") == "REJECTED"
            and ack_body.get("error") == "stale_result"
            and row.get("regime") == "stale"
            and isinstance(case_id, str)
            and isinstance(fixture_ref, str)
            and isinstance(trace, dict)
            and trace.get("status") == "STALE"
            and fixture_ref.endswith(f"#{case_id}")
            and bool(controls)
        )
        if verified_stale:
            stale_verified_rows += 1
            stale_arm_counts[str(row.get("arm"))] += 1
            stale_control_counts.update(controls)
            stale_case_controls.setdefault(case_id, set()).update(controls)
    stale_disclosure = {
        "verified_rows": stale_verified_rows,
        "assigned_failure_rows": assigned_failure_rows,
        "by_arm": dict(stale_arm_counts),
        "by_control": dict(stale_control_counts),
        "by_case": {case_id: sorted(controls) for case_id, controls in stale_case_controls.items()},
        "unclassified_rows": assigned_failure_rows - stale_verified_rows,
    }
    for row in p_rows:
        attempts = row.get("cli_exec_attempts")
        _require(
            isinstance(attempts, list) and len(attempts) == 1,
            "NPC P requires one observed CLI exec attempt per assignment",
        )
        attempt = attempts[0]
        _require(
            isinstance(attempt, dict)
            and attempt.get("schema_version") == "codex-exec-attempt/1"
            and attempt.get("operation") == "exec"
            and attempt.get("runner_injected") is False
            and attempt.get("tool_policy_enforced") is True
            and attempt.get("request_id") == row.get("request", {}).get("request_id")
            and isinstance(attempt.get("disabled_feature_count"), int)
            and not isinstance(attempt.get("disabled_feature_count"), bool)
            and attempt["disabled_feature_count"] > 0,
            "NPC P CLI attempt provenance mismatch",
        )
        _require(row.get("model_calls") == len(attempts), "NPC P observed attempt counter mismatch")
        _require(
            not any(failure.get("code") == "model_skipped" for failure in row.get("failure", [])),
            "NPC skipped P is not publishable",
        )
    assignments = manifest.get("assignments")
    _require(
        isinstance(assignments, list) and len(assignments) == 72,
        "NPC assignment manifest cardinality drift",
    )
    _require(isinstance(aggregate.get("arms"), dict), "NPC aggregate arms missing")
    for arm in ARMS:
        summary = aggregate["arms"].get(arm)
        _require(isinstance(summary, dict), f"NPC arm summary missing: {arm}")
        _require(summary.get("n") == 24, f"NPC arm denominator drift: {arm}")
        _require(
            summary.get("source_independent_n") == 0, f"NPC source-independent count drift: {arm}"
        )
    usage = aggregate.get("usage", {})
    _require(
        usage.get("token_accounting_available") is False, "NPC token availability boundary drift"
    )
    _require(
        usage.get("input_tokens") is None and usage.get("output_tokens") is None,
        "NPC token NA drift",
    )
    _require(usage.get("cost") is None, "NPC cost NA drift")
    _require(
        usage.get("model_revision_available") is False, "NPC model revision availability drift"
    )
    _require(usage.get("model_revision") is None, "NPC model revision NA drift")
    claims = aggregate.get("claims", {})
    for key, expected in {
        "independent_labels": False,
        "efficacy_pending": True,
        "superiority": False,
        "generalization": False,
        "human_benefit": False,
        "c_result_promotion": False,
    }.items():
        _require(claims.get(key) is expected, f"NPC claim boundary drift: {key}")
    _require(isinstance(replay, dict), "NPC replay result must be an object")
    _require(replay.get("model_calls") == 0, "NPC replay unexpectedly invoked a model")
    return {
        "aggregate": aggregate,
        "manifest": manifest,
        "engineering": engineering,
        "screening": screening,
        "replay": replay,
        "model_metadata": model_metadata,
        "stale_disclosure": stale_disclosure,
        "validation_reports": validation_reports,
        "receipts": receipts,
    }


def _latex(value: Any) -> str:
    text = str(value)
    for old, new in (
        ("\\", r"\textbackslash{}"),
        ("_", r"\_"),
        ("%", r"\%"),
        ("&", r"\&"),
        ("#", r"\#"),
    ):
        text = text.replace(old, new)
    return text


def _status_counts(rows: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> Counter[str]:
    values: Counter[str] = Counter()
    for row in rows:
        status = row.get("status") or row.get("result", {}).get("status") or row.get("outcome")
        values[str(status or "UNSPECIFIED")] += 1
    return values


def _failure_counts(summary: dict[str, Any]) -> Counter[str]:
    values = summary.get("failures_by_code", {})
    _require(isinstance(values, dict), "NPC failures_by_code must be an object")
    return Counter({str(key): int(value) for key, value in values.items()})


def _results(packet: dict[str, Any], korean: bool) -> str:
    aggregate = packet["aggregate"]
    eng_n = len(packet["engineering"])
    eng_status = ", ".join(
        f"{_latex(key)}={value}"
        for key, value in sorted(_status_counts(packet["engineering"]).items())
    )
    model_metadata = packet["model_metadata"]
    requested_model = model_metadata["requested"]
    returned_revision = model_metadata["returned"]
    unknown_returned_count = int(model_metadata.get("returned_unknown_count", 0))
    cohort_size = int(model_metadata.get("cohort_size", 24))
    unknown_label = "알 수 없음 (null)" if korean else "unknown (null)"
    requested_label = unknown_label if requested_model is None else _latex(requested_model)
    returned_label = unknown_label if returned_revision is None else _latex(returned_revision)
    if returned_revision is not None and unknown_returned_count:
        partial_word = "부분" if korean else "partial"
        unknown_word = "알 수 없음" if korean else "unknown"
        returned_label = (
            f"{returned_label} ({partial_word}; {unknown_word} "
            f"{unknown_returned_count}/{cohort_size})"
        )
    scr_status = ", ".join(
        f"{_latex(key)}={value}"
        for key, value in sorted(_status_counts(packet["screening"]).items())
    )
    p_rows = [row for row in packet["screening"] if row["arm"] == "P"]
    p_attempts = sum(row["model_calls"] for row in p_rows)
    p_decisions = sum(isinstance(row.get("decision"), dict) for row in p_rows)
    p_failures = sum(row["status"] == "assigned_failure" for row in p_rows)
    stale_disclosure = packet["stale_disclosure"]
    stale_arm_label = ", ".join(
        f"{arm}={int(stale_disclosure.get('by_arm', {}).get(arm, 0))}" for arm in ARMS
    )
    stale_case_label = (
        ", ".join(
            f"{_latex(case_id)} ({', '.join(_latex(control) for control in controls)})"
            for case_id, controls in sorted(stale_disclosure.get("by_case", {}).items())
        )
        or "none"
    )
    stale_verified_rows = int(stale_disclosure.get("verified_rows", 0))
    unclassified_failure_rows = int(stale_disclosure.get("unclassified_rows", 0))
    if korean:
        stale_failure_disclosure = (
            f"검증된 사전 작성 stale-control ACK 거부는 arm별 {stale_arm_label}건"
            f"({stale_case_label})이며, {stale_verified_rows}개 인스턴스 모두 "
            f"대응하는 음성 대조 gate가 모두 통과했다. 따라서 이 행들은 assigned failure로 "
            f"분모에 남기되 method-performance 결과로 해석하지 않으며, 기타 assigned failure "
            f"{unclassified_failure_rows}개는 미분류로 남긴다."
        )
    else:
        stale_failure_disclosure = (
            f"Verified pre-authored stale-control ACK rejections are {stale_arm_label} per arm"
            f" ({stale_case_label}); {stale_verified_rows} instances had all "
            f"corresponding negative-control gates passed. These rows remain assigned failures in the "
            f"denominator, not method-performance outcomes; other assigned failures "
            f"({unclassified_failure_rows}) remain unclassified."
        )
    if korean:
        return (
            rf"""
\subsection{{고정 목표 NPC 운영 (NPC-ENG1/NPC-SCR1)}}
Mira의 고정 목표는 항구 신호 복구 후 허가된 조석 단서를 제공하는 것이다. Python은 typed intent와 공개 관측으로 bounded BFS 계획을 계산한다. 획득·설치는 플레이어가, 허가된 \texttt{{reveal\_hint}}는 NPC가 수행한다. Godot은 session/epoch/request/goal/policy/revision/hash를 동기 검사한 뒤 기존 writer를 await 없이 호출한다. 결과와 새 관측이 다음 계획을 결정하므로 단일 후보의 validator-error repair와 구별된다.

NPC-ENG1은 실제 엔진 snapshot에서 시작한 R arm의 결정론적 engineering 사례 {eng_n}개({eng_status})다. NPC-SCR1은 P/R/O 각 24개, 전체 {aggregate["assignment_count"]}개 배정과 실패를 모두 보존한다({scr_status}). P는 인증된 Codex CLI 실행 시도 {p_attempts}회, 반환 decision {p_decisions}개, 배정 실패 {p_failures}개를 기록했다. 이는 원격 과금 호출 횟수가 아니다. 요청 모델: {requested_label}; decision에 기록된 모델 식별자: {returned_label}. 후자는 설정된 selector이며 provider revision이 아니다. provider revision은 사용할 수 없고 null 식별자는 미상으로 남긴다. 전역 설정은 모델 식별 근거가 아니다. R은 작성된 규칙을 사용한다. O는 \texttt{{same\_model\_family\_auxiliary}}로 선언된 protocol auxiliary일 뿐이며 실제 모델 계열 일치는 unverified(검증되지 않음)이다. 독립 원본은 0개이고 O는 oracle이나 semantic gold가 아니다. 표~\ref{{tab:npc-screening}}과 그림~\ref{{fig:npc-screening}}은 bounded protocol-conformance 고정 사례의 기술적 계수이지 회복 효능·우월성·일반화 추정이 아니다. efficacy는 pending이며 토큰·비용은 미측정이다. 읽기 전용 CLI와 격리 작업 디렉터리를 사용했지만 tool-free 추론이나 적대적 기밀성은 검증하지 않았다.
{stale_failure_disclosure}

""".strip()
            + "\n"
        )
    return (
        rf"""
\subsection{{Fixed-Goal NPC Operation (NPC-ENG1/NPC-SCR1)}}
Mira's fixed goal is to deliver the authorized tide lead after harbor-signal restoration. Python computes bounded BFS plans from typed intent and public observations. Acquire/install remain player actions; the NPC may only commit the authorized \texttt{{reveal\_hint}}. Godot synchronously checks session/epoch/request/goal/policy/revision/hash before calling the existing writer without an await. Results and new observations determine the next plan, unlike single-candidate validator-error repair.

NPC-ENG1 comprises {eng_n} deterministic R-arm engineering cases initialized from actual engine snapshots ({eng_status}). NPC-SCR1 retains all {aggregate["assignment_count"]} assignments and failures, 24 per P/R/O arm ({scr_status}). P records {p_attempts} authenticated Codex CLI exec attempts, {p_decisions} returned decisions, and {p_failures} assigned failures; these are not remote billing-call counts. Requested model: {requested_label}; recorded decision-model identifier: {returned_label}. The latter propagates the configured selector, not a provider revision; the provider revision is unavailable. Null identifiers remain unknown; global defaults are not evidence. R uses authored rules. O is a protocol-declared auxiliary under \texttt{{same\_model\_family\_auxiliary}}. Actual model-family matching is unverified. No source is independent; O is neither an oracle nor semantic gold. Table~\ref{{tab:npc-screening}} and Fig.~\ref{{fig:npc-screening}} report bounded protocol-conformance fixed-case counts, not recovery-efficacy, superiority, or generalization estimates. Efficacy remains pending; tokens and cost are unmeasured. A read-only CLI and isolated working directory were used, but tool-free inference and adversarial confidentiality were not established.
{stale_failure_disclosure}
""".strip()
        + "\n"
    )


def _tables(packet: dict[str, Any], korean: bool) -> str:
    aggregate = packet["aggregate"]
    caption = (
        "NPC-SCR1 고정 사례 실행 계수. NPC-ENG1의 16개 사례와 분리하며 모든 실패를 분모에 보존한다."
        if korean
        else "NPC-SCR1 fixed-case execution counts, separate from the 16 NPC-ENG1 cases; all assigned failures remain in the denominator."
    )
    headers = (
        r"Arm & 배정 & 완료/실패 & 목표 도달 & 회복 E/U \\"
        if korean
        else r"Arm & Assigned & Complete/fail & Goal reached & Recovery E/U \\"
    )
    rows = []
    for arm in ARMS:
        summary = aggregate["arms"][arm]
        observed = []
        for key in ("goal_reached_n", "recovery_eligible_n", "recovery_unreachable_n"):
            value = summary.get(key)
            _require(
                value is None or (type(value) is int and 0 <= value <= summary["n"]),
                f"invalid observed count: {arm}/{key}",
            )
            observed.append(str(value) if value is not None else "NA")
        goal, eligible, unreachable = observed
        rows.append(
            f"{arm} & {summary['n']} & {summary['completed_n']}/{summary['assigned_failure_n']} & {goal} & {eligible}/{unreachable} "
            + r"\\"
        )
    note = (
        "E: 회복 평가 가능, U: 도달 불가; NA: 미측정. 목표 관측 계수는 회복률이 아니다. Replay 적합성은 실패 행을 완료로 바꾸지 않는다. O의 독립 원본은 0개다."
        if korean
        else "E: recovery eligible; U: unreachable; NA: unmeasured. Observed goal counts are not recovery rates. Valid replay does not turn a failed assignment into a completed one. O has zero independent sources."
    )
    return (
        rf"""
\begin{{table}}[t]
\caption{{{caption}}}
\label{{tab:npc-screening}}
\centering\scriptsize
\setlength{{\tabcolsep}}{{2.8pt}}
\renewcommand{{\arraystretch}}{{1.12}}
\begin{{tabularx}}{{\columnwidth}}{{@{{}}l r >{{\centering\arraybackslash}}X >{{\centering\arraybackslash}}X >{{\centering\arraybackslash}}X@{{}}}}
\toprule
{headers}
\midrule
{chr(10).join(rows)}
\bottomrule
\end{{tabularx}}
\vspace{{1pt}}\parbox{{0.97\columnwidth}}{{\scriptsize {note}}}
\end{{table}}
""".strip()
        + "\n"
    )


def _svg(packet: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    arms = packet["aggregate"]["arms"]
    # Keep the source aspect stable through the repository's rsvg-convert → EPS →
    # ps2pdf pipeline: 1100×468 SVG pixels become 825×351 PDF points without
    # independent rounding changing the aspect ratio.
    width, height = 1100, 468
    parts = [
        '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>',
        f'<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="{width}" height="{height}" viewBox="0 0 {width} {height}" role="img" aria-labelledby="svg-title svg-desc">',
        '<title id="svg-title">NPC-SCR1 fixed-case trace outcomes</title>',
        '<desc id="svg-desc">Observed completed traces and retained assigned failures for 24 rows per arm in authored fixed cases, including Codex-backed P. These descriptive counts are not estimates of recovery efficacy. O is a protocol-declared auxiliary; actual model-family matching is unverified.</desc>',
        "<style>text{font-family:Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif;fill:#202124}.title{font-size:32px;font-weight:700}.label{font-size:30px;font-weight:700}.body{font-size:28px}.note{font-size:28px;fill:#5F6368}.connector{fill:none}</style>",
        f'<rect width="{width}" height="{height}" fill="white"/>',
        '<text x="40" y="48" class="title">NPC-SCR1 | FIXED-CASE TRACE OUTCOMES</text>',
        '<text x="40" y="90" class="note">P: Codex intent | R: authored rules | O: protocol auxiliary (family unverified)</text>',
        '<rect x="145" y="115" width="25" height="22" fill="#0072B2"/>',
        '<text x="183" y="135" class="note">Completed trace</text>',
        '<rect x="470" y="115" width="25" height="22" fill="#D55E00"/>',
        '<text x="508" y="135" class="note">Assigned failure (retained)</text>',
    ]
    goal_counts = []
    for index, arm in enumerate(ARMS):
        summary = arms[arm]
        assigned = summary["n"]
        completed = summary["completed_n"]
        failed = summary["assigned_failure_n"]
        _require(type(assigned) is int and assigned == 24, f"invalid assigned count: {arm}")
        _require(type(completed) is int and type(failed) is int, f"unmeasured trace count: {arm}")
        _require(
            completed >= 0 and failed >= 0 and completed + failed == assigned,
            f"incomplete trace accounting: {arm}",
        )
        y = 165 + index * 65
        completed_width = 540 * completed / assigned
        failed_width = 540 * failed / assigned
        parts += [
            f'<text x="70" y="{y + 28}" class="label">{arm}</text>',
            f'<rect class="bar completed" x="145" y="{y}" width="{completed_width:g}" height="34" fill="#0072B2"/>',
            f'<rect class="bar assigned-failure" x="{145 + completed_width:g}" y="{y}" width="{failed_width:g}" height="34" fill="#D55E00"/>',
            f'<line class="connector" x1="685" y1="{y + 17}" x2="705" y2="{y + 17}" stroke="#C9CDD1" stroke-width="2"/>',
            f'<text x="715" y="{y + 28}" class="body">{completed} complete / {failed} failed</text>',
        ]
        reached = summary.get("goal_reached_n")
        goal_counts.append(f"{arm}: {reached}/{assigned}" if type(reached) is int else f"{arm}: NA")
    goal_text = escape("Goal reached, fixed cases only | " + " | ".join(goal_counts))
    parts += [
        f'<text x="40" y="389" class="note">{goal_text}</text>',
        '<text x="40" y="427" class="note">Independent-source rows: 0. No efficacy, superiority, or generalization claim.</text>',
        "</svg>",
    ]
    return "\n".join(parts) + "\n"


def main() -> None:
    packet = _load_packet()
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    FIGURES.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (OUT / "npc_validation_reports.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(packet["validation_reports"], ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    for language, korean in (("en", False), ("ko", True)):
        (OUT / f"npc_results_{language}.tex").write_text(
            _results(packet, korean), encoding="utf-8", newline="\n"
        )
        (OUT / f"npc_tables_{language}.tex").write_text(
            _tables(packet, korean), encoding="utf-8", newline="\n"
        )
    target = FIGURES / "fig_npc_screening.svg"
    target.write_text(_svg(packet), encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
    print(
        f"generated NPC paper fragments and {target.relative_to(ROOT)} from {PACKET.relative_to(ROOT)}"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== VERBATIM SOURCE FILE: game-track/godot/scripts/canonical_state.gd | SHA256 6a4eb4693e0c34c24399e2d23ace4aceebb45fe9eb54e410bb25708232e88a58 | 1322 bytes =====
class_name ExperimentalCanonicalState
extends RefCounted

## Canonical JSON used only for deterministic state and artifact-integrity hashes.
## This is an unkeyed SHA-256 checksum, not authentication or writer identity.

static func canonicalize(value: Variant) -> Variant:
	if value is Dictionary:
		var ordered := {}
		var keys: Array = value.keys()
		keys.sort()
		for key in keys:
			ordered[str(key)] = canonicalize(value[key])
		return ordered
	if value is Array:
		var items: Array = []
		for item in value:
			items.append(canonicalize(item))
		return items
	return value


static func canonical_json(value: Variant) -> String:
	return JSON.stringify(canonicalize(value), "", false)


static func sha256(value: Variant) -> String:
	var bytes := canonical_json(value).to_utf8_buffer()
	var context := HashingContext.new()
	var start_error := context.start(HashingContext.HASH_SHA256)
	if start_error != OK:
		push_error("cannot initialize SHA-256 context")
		return ""
	var update_error := context.update(bytes)
	if update_error != OK:
		push_error("cannot update SHA-256 context")
		return ""
	return context.finish().hex_encode()


static func sorted_unique(values: Array) -> Array:
	var index := {}
	for value in values:
		index[str(value)] = true
	var result: Array = index.keys()
	result.sort()
	return result

===== VERBATIM SOURCE FILE: game-track/godot/scripts/game3d/npc_agent_client.gd | SHA256 e56911b029a372017c7046a5b1bb0017156275634c7e86b704169f5fed124989 | 33610 bytes =====
class_name NpcAgentClient
extends Node

## Loopback-only asynchronous client for the fixed-goal NPC service.
## Python can interpret and plan, but this node never writes canonical state.
## Godot remains the sole authority for proposal validation and world commits.

const CanonicalState = preload("res://scripts/canonical_state.gd")

signal decision_received(envelope: Dictionary, context: Dictionary)
signal result_recorded(envelope: Dictionary, context: Dictionary)
signal directive_received(code: String, payload: Dictionary)
signal status_changed(text: String)
signal protocol_error(reason: String)

const SCHEMA_VERSION := "npc-goal-recovery/1.1.0"
const SCENARIO_ID := "sealed-lighthouse-v1"
const NPC_ID := "captain_mira"
const GOAL_ID := "harbor-signal-restored-authorized-tide-lead-v1"
const GOAL_VERSION := "1"
const ENDPOINT := "http://127.0.0.1:8768"
const BODY_LIMIT_BYTES := 65536
const INTENT_DEADLINE_MS := 30000
const EPISODE_DEADLINE_MS := 900000
const MAX_INTENT_CALLS := 8
const MAX_ACTION_ATTEMPTS := 32
const MAX_REPLANS := 16
const MAX_PLAN_EXPANSIONS := 32
const MAX_INFLIGHT := 1
const MAX_UTTERANCE_CHARS := 1000

var _http: HTTPRequest
var _game: Node
var _ui: Node
var _session_id := ""
var _episode_id := ""
var _session_epoch := 0
var _sequence := 0
var _delivery_index := 0
var _request_generation := 0
var _intent_calls := 0
var _episode_started_ms := 0
var _pending: Dictionary = {}
var _last_decision_context: Dictionary = {}
var _issued_actions: Dictionary = {}
var _policy_hash := ""
var _config_hash := ""
var _capability := ""
var _closed := false
var _engineering_mode := false
var _interpretation_accepted := false


func _ready() -> void:
	_http = HTTPRequest.new()
	_http.timeout = float(INTENT_DEADLINE_MS) / 1000.0
	_http.body_size_limit = BODY_LIMIT_BYTES
	_http.max_redirects = 0
	_http.request_completed.connect(_on_request_completed)
	add_child(_http)
	_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | READY")


func setup(game: Node, ui: Node) -> void:
	_game = game
	_ui = ui
	_capability = OS.get_environment("TRACE_RPG_NPC_CAPABILITY").strip_edges()
	_policy_hash = str(_game.npc_policy_hash())
	_config_hash = str(_game.npc_config_hash())
	_start_session()


func set_capability(value: String) -> void:
	_capability = value.strip_edges()

func bind_engineering_context(context: Dictionary, current_state_ref: Dictionary, deadline_ms: int = INTENT_DEADLINE_MS) -> Dictionary:
	var args := OS.get_cmdline_user_args()
	if "--npc-experiment" not in args and "--npc-bootstrap" not in args:
		return {"accepted": false, "codes": ["ENGINEERING_MODE_REQUIRED"]}
	if _game == null or context.is_empty():
		return {"accepted": false, "codes": ["ENGINEERING_CONTEXT_INVALID"]}
	for key in ["session_id", "episode_id", "session_epoch", "request_id", "goal_id", "goal_version"]:
		if not context.has(key) or str(context.get(key, "")).is_empty():
			return {"accepted": false, "codes": ["MISSING_CONTEXT_" + key.to_upper()]}
	if str(context.get("goal_id", "")) != GOAL_ID or str(context.get("goal_version", "")) != GOAL_VERSION:
		return {"accepted": false, "codes": ["GOAL_MISMATCH"]}
	if context.has("policy_hash") and not str(context.get("policy_hash", "")).is_empty() \
		and str(context.get("policy_hash", "")) != _policy_hash:
		return {"accepted": false, "codes": ["POLICY_HASH_MISMATCH"]}
	if context.has("config_hash") and not str(context.get("config_hash", "")).is_empty() \
		and str(context.get("config_hash", "")) != _config_hash:
		return {"accepted": false, "codes": ["CONFIG_IDENTITY_MISMATCH"]}
	_session_id = str(context.get("session_id", ""))
	_episode_id = str(context.get("episode_id", ""))
	_session_epoch = int(context.get("session_epoch", -1))
	_request_generation += 1
	_sequence = 0
	_delivery_index = 0
	_last_decision_context.clear()
	_issued_actions.clear()
	_interpretation_accepted = false
	_closed = false
	_engineering_mode = true
	var bound := context.duplicate(true)
	bound["generation"] = _request_generation
	bound["policy_hash"] = _policy_hash
	bound["config_hash"] = _config_hash
	bound["deadline_ms"] = Time.get_ticks_msec() + (deadline_ms if deadline_ms > 0 else -1)
	bound["expected_revision"] = int(current_state_ref.get("revision", -1))
	bound["expected_sha256"] = str(current_state_ref.get("sha256", ""))
	bound["state_ref"] = {"revision": bound["expected_revision"], "sha256": bound["expected_sha256"]}
	return {"accepted": true, "context": bound}


func has_accepted_interpretation() -> bool:
	return _interpretation_accepted


func _start_session() -> void:
	_request_generation += 1
	_cancel_http_if_pending("session_reset")
	var seed := "%s:%s:%s" % [Time.get_ticks_usec(), get_instance_id(), OS.get_process_id()]
	_session_id = "npc-%s" % CanonicalState.sha256(seed).substr(0, 24)
	_episode_id = "episode-%s" % CanonicalState.sha256(_session_id).substr(0, 24)
	_episode_started_ms = Time.get_ticks_msec()
	_session_epoch = 0
	_sequence = 0
	_delivery_index = 0
	_intent_calls = 0
	_pending.clear()
	_last_decision_context.clear()
	_issued_actions.clear()
	_interpretation_accepted = false
	_closed = false


func launch_identity() -> Dictionary:
	return {
		"session_id": _session_id,
		"episode_id": _episode_id,
		"session_epoch": _session_epoch,
		"policy_hash": _policy_hash,
		"config_hash": _config_hash,
	}


func begin_dialogue() -> void:
	_closed = false
	_session_epoch += 1
	_request_generation += 1
	_cancel_http_if_pending("dialogue_open")
	_last_decision_context.clear()
	_issued_actions.clear()
	_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | READY")


func invalidate_pending(reason: String = "invalidated") -> void:
	_session_epoch += 1
	_request_generation += 1
	_cancel_http_if_pending(reason)
	_last_decision_context.clear()
	_issued_actions.clear()
	if reason != "dialogue_closed":
		_interpretation_accepted = false
	_closed = reason in ["dialogue_closed", "exit", "close"]
	_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | CANCELLED")

func request_utterance(utterance: String, observation: Dictionary) -> bool:
	var text := utterance.strip_edges()
	if text.is_empty():
		_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | ENTER TEXT")
		return false
	if text.length() > MAX_UTTERANCE_CHARS:
		_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | TEXT TOO LONG")
		return false
	if _intent_calls >= MAX_INTENT_CALLS:
		_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | CALL BUDGET REACHED")
		return false
	if Time.get_ticks_msec() - _episode_started_ms > EPISODE_DEADLINE_MS:
		_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | EPISODE TIMEOUT")
		return false
	# A newer utterance is an explicit epoch boundary. Cancel before replacing
	# pending state so any late transport callback is tombstoned.
	_session_epoch += 1
	_request_generation += 1
	_cancel_http_if_pending("newer_utterance")
	_last_decision_context.clear()
	_issued_actions.clear()
	_interpretation_accepted = false
	_intent_calls += 1
	return _request_step("utterance", text, observation)


func request_observation(observation: Dictionary) -> bool:
	# Result feedback is observation-only: the service reuses its last typed
	# intent and planner context, so this path never spends another model call.
	if _closed or _game == null or not _interpretation_accepted:
		return false
	if not _pending.is_empty():
		_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | BUSY")
		return false
	if Time.get_ticks_msec() - _episode_started_ms > EPISODE_DEADLINE_MS:
		_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | EPISODE TIMEOUT")
		return false
	return _request_step("observation", "", observation)


func _request_step(event_kind: String, utterance: String, observation: Dictionary) -> bool:
	if _game == null:
		_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | UNAVAILABLE")
		return false
	if not _pending.is_empty():
		_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | BUSY")
		return false
	var state_ref: Dictionary = _game.npc_state_ref()
	var request_id := _new_id("request")
	var body := {
		"event_kind": event_kind,
		"utterance": utterance if event_kind == "utterance" else null,
		"observation": _sanitize_observation(observation),
	}
	var envelope := _build_envelope(
		"request",
		request_id,
		body,
		state_ref,
		_sha256_utf8(utterance) if event_kind == "utterance" else "",
	)
	_pending = {
		"kind": "step",
		"event_kind": event_kind,
		"generation": _request_generation,
		"session_epoch": _session_epoch,
		"session_id": _session_id,
		"episode_id": _episode_id,
		"goal_id": GOAL_ID,
		"goal_version": GOAL_VERSION,
		"request_id": request_id,
		"expected_revision": int(state_ref.get("revision", -1)),
		"expected_sha256": str(state_ref.get("sha256", "")),
		"deadline_ms": Time.get_ticks_msec() + INTENT_DEADLINE_MS,
		"policy_hash": _policy_hash,
		"config_hash": _config_hash,
	}
	_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | WAITING")
	var sent := _send("/npc/step", envelope)
	if not sent:
		_pending.clear()
		_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | UNAVAILABLE")
	return sent


func accept_decision(envelope: Dictionary, context: Dictionary, current_state_ref: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var errors := _validate_decision(envelope, context, current_state_ref)
	if not errors.is_empty():
		return {"accepted": false, "codes": errors}
	var body: Dictionary = envelope["body"]
	var steps: Array = body.get("steps", [])
	if steps.is_empty():
		var terminal_status := str(body.get("status", "ERROR"))
		if terminal_status not in ["NO_ROUTE", "GOAL_REACHED", "ERROR"]:
			return {"accepted": false, "codes": ["MISSING_PLAN_STEP"]}
		if terminal_status == "GOAL_REACHED" and not bool(_game.npc_public_observation().get("goal_reached", false)):
			return {"accepted": false, "kind": "none", "status": "GOAL_NOT_REACHED", "codes": ["GOAL_NOT_REACHED"]}
		directive_received.emit("NPC_" + terminal_status, {"status": terminal_status})
		_interpretation_accepted = true
		return {"accepted": true, "kind": "none", "status": terminal_status}
	var step: Dictionary = steps[0]
	var operation := str(step.get("operation", ""))
	var execution_mode := str(step.get("execution_mode", ""))
	var action_id := str(step.get("action_id", ""))
	if action_id.is_empty() or _issued_actions.has(action_id):
		return {"accepted": false, "codes": ["DUPLICATE_ACTION"]}
	var arguments: Dictionary = step.get("arguments", {})
	var observation := _sanitize_observation(_game.npc_public_observation())
	if operation == "reveal_hint":
		if execution_mode != "commit_world":
			return {"accepted": false, "codes": ["EXECUTION_MODE_MISMATCH"]}
		if arguments != {"actor_id": NPC_ID, "fact_id": "tide_marks_hint"}:
			return {"accepted": false, "codes": ["REVEAL_BINDING_MISMATCH"]}
		# Mira may reveal only the authorized, known tide lead. The existing hard
		# writer remains the final synchronous authority after this gate.
		if not bool(observation.get("signal_restored", false)) \
			or not bool(observation.get("tide_lead_authorized", false)) \
			or not bool(observation.get("in_dialogue", false)) \
			or bool(observation.get("goal_reached", false)):
			return {"accepted": false, "codes": ["REVEAL_PRECONDITION_MISMATCH"]}
	elif operation in ["acquire_object", "install_lens"]:
		if execution_mode != "player_request":
			return {"accepted": false, "codes": ["EXECUTION_MODE_MISMATCH"]}
		if arguments != {"object_id": "signal_lens"}:
			return {"accepted": false, "codes": ["PLAYER_ACTION_BINDING_MISMATCH"]}
		if operation == "acquire_object" and (not bool(observation.get("lens_reachable", false)) or bool(observation.get("lens_carried", false))):
			return {"accepted": false, "codes": ["PLAYER_ACTION_PRECONDITION_MISMATCH"]}
		if operation == "install_lens" and (not bool(observation.get("lens_carried", false)) or bool(observation.get("signal_restored", false))):
			return {"accepted": false, "codes": ["PLAYER_ACTION_PRECONDITION_MISMATCH"]}
	else:
		if operation not in ["clarify", "negotiate"] or execution_mode != "dialogue":
			return {"accepted": false, "codes": ["UNSUPPORTED_STEP"]}
		return _accept_dialogue_step(operation)
	var issued := context.duplicate(true)
	issued["action_id"] = action_id
	issued["operation"] = operation
	issued["prior_state_ref"] = {"revision": int(current_state_ref.get("revision", -1)), "sha256": str(current_state_ref.get("sha256", ""))}
	issued["result_submitted"] = false
	issued["intent_id"] = str(body.get("intent_id", ""))
	_issued_actions[action_id] = issued
	var directive_context := issued.duplicate(true)
	if operation == "reveal_hint":
		directive_received.emit("NPC_REVEAL_HINT", {"action_id": action_id, "context": directive_context})
		_interpretation_accepted = true
		return {"accepted": true, "kind": "reveal_hint", "action_id": action_id, "context": directive_context}
	directive_received.emit("NPC_PLAYER_ACTION", {"operation": operation, "action_id": action_id, "context": directive_context})
	_interpretation_accepted = true
	return {"accepted": true, "kind": "player_request", "operation": operation, "action_id": action_id, "context": directive_context}


func _accept_dialogue_step(operation: String) -> Dictionary:
	_interpretation_accepted = true
	directive_received.emit("NPC_DIALOGUE", {"operation": operation})
	return {"accepted": true, "kind": "dialogue", "operation": operation}


func validate_action_context(context: Dictionary, action_id: String, operation: String, current_state_ref: Dictionary, require_original_state: bool = true) -> Array:
	var errors: Array = []
	if context.is_empty() or not _issued_actions.has(action_id):
		errors.append("ISSUED_ACTION_UNKNOWN")
	else:
		var issued: Dictionary = _issued_actions[action_id]
		if str(issued.get("action_id", "")) != action_id or str(issued.get("operation", "")) != operation:
			errors.append("ISSUED_ACTION_BINDING_MISMATCH")
		if str(context.get("operation", operation)) != operation or str(context.get("action_id", action_id)) != action_id:
			errors.append("ISSUED_ACTION_BINDING_MISMATCH")
		if str(issued.get("request_id", "")) != str(context.get("request_id", "")):
			errors.append("ISSUED_ACTION_BINDING_MISMATCH")
		for key in ["session_id", "episode_id", "session_epoch", "generation", "policy_hash", "config_hash", "deadline_ms"]:
			if str(issued.get(key, "")) != str(context.get(key, "")):
				errors.append("ISSUED_ACTION_BINDING_MISMATCH")
		if require_original_state:
			if int(issued.get("expected_revision", -1)) != int(context.get("expected_revision", -2)) \
				or str(issued.get("expected_sha256", "")) != str(context.get("expected_sha256", "")):
				errors.append("ISSUED_ACTION_BINDING_MISMATCH")
		if bool(issued.get("result_submitted", false)):
			errors.append("DUPLICATE_ACTION")
	if int(context.get("session_epoch", -1)) != _session_epoch or int(context.get("generation", -1)) != _request_generation:
		errors.append("EPOCH_OR_GENERATION_MISMATCH")
	if str(context.get("session_id", _session_id)) != _session_id or str(context.get("episode_id", _episode_id)) != _episode_id:
		errors.append("SESSION_BINDING_MISMATCH")
	if str(context.get("policy_hash", "")) != _policy_hash or str(context.get("config_hash", "")) != _config_hash:
		errors.append("CONFIG_IDENTITY_MISMATCH")
	if Time.get_ticks_msec() > int(context.get("deadline_ms", 0)):
		errors.append("DEADLINE_EXPIRED")
	if int(current_state_ref.get("revision", -1)) != int(context.get("expected_revision", -2)):
		errors.append("CURRENT_REVISION_CHANGED")
	if str(current_state_ref.get("sha256", "")) != str(context.get("expected_sha256", "")):
		errors.append("CURRENT_STATE_CHANGED")
	return CanonicalState.sorted_unique(errors)


func submit_action_result(context: Dictionary, action_id: String, operation: String, status: String, feedback: String, next_state_ref: Dictionary = {}) -> bool:
	if _closed or context.is_empty() or _game == null or not _pending.is_empty():
		return false
	var current_ref: Dictionary = _game.npc_state_ref()
	var check_context := context.duplicate(true)
	# The result is emitted after the synchronous hard writer. Validate all
	# session/action identity against the post-write reference while retaining
	# the original prior_state_ref in the issued action context.
	check_context["expected_revision"] = int(current_ref.get("revision", -1))
	check_context["expected_sha256"] = str(current_ref.get("sha256", ""))
	var binding_errors := validate_action_context(check_context, action_id, operation, current_ref, false)
	if not binding_errors.is_empty():
		protocol_error.emit("RESULT_BINDING_REJECTED|" + "|".join(binding_errors))
		return false
	if status not in ["APPLIED", "REJECTED", "STALE", "DUPLICATE", "CANCELLED", "ERROR"]:
		protocol_error.emit("RESULT_STATUS_INVALID")
		return false
	var observation: Dictionary = _sanitize_observation(_game.npc_public_observation())
	var body := {
		"action_id": action_id,
		"operation": operation,
		"status": status,
		"engine_revision": int(current_ref.get("revision", 0)),
		"state_ref": current_ref,
		"feedback": feedback.substr(0, 512),
		"observation": observation,
	}
	var envelope := _build_envelope("result", str(context.get("request_id", "")), body, current_ref, "")
	var issued: Dictionary = _issued_actions.get(action_id, {})
	issued["result_submitted"] = true
	issued["next_state_ref"] = current_ref.duplicate(true)
	_issued_actions[action_id] = issued
	if _engineering_mode:
		return true
	_pending = {
		"kind": "result",
		"generation": _request_generation,
		"session_epoch": _session_epoch,
		"session_id": _session_id,
		"episode_id": _episode_id,
		"request_id": str(context.get("request_id", "")),
		"expected_revision": int(current_ref.get("revision", -1)),
		"expected_sha256": str(current_ref.get("sha256", "")),
		"deadline_ms": Time.get_ticks_msec() + INTENT_DEADLINE_MS,
		"policy_hash": _policy_hash,
		"config_hash": _config_hash,
		"action_id": action_id,
		"operation": operation,
		"prior_state_ref": context.get("prior_state_ref", {}).duplicate(true) if context.get("prior_state_ref", {}) is Dictionary else {},
		"next_state_ref": current_ref.duplicate(true),
	}
	_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | CONFIRMING")
	var sent := _send("/npc/result", envelope)
	if not sent:
		_pending.clear()
		issued["result_submitted"] = false
		_issued_actions[action_id] = issued
		_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | UNAVAILABLE")
	return sent


func _validate_decision(envelope: Dictionary, context: Dictionary, current_state_ref: Dictionary) -> Array:
	var errors := _validate_common(envelope, "decision")
	if context.is_empty():
		errors.append("MISSING_CONTEXT")
	else:
		if str(envelope.get("request_id", "")) != str(context.get("request_id", "")):
			errors.append("REQUEST_MISMATCH")
		if int(envelope.get("session_epoch", -1)) != int(context.get("session_epoch", -2)):
			errors.append("EPOCH_MISMATCH")
		if int(context.get("generation", -1)) != _request_generation:
			errors.append("GENERATION_MISMATCH")
		if str(context.get("session_id", "")) != _session_id or str(context.get("episode_id", "")) != _episode_id:
			errors.append("SESSION_BINDING_MISMATCH")
		if str(context.get("goal_id", "")) != GOAL_ID or str(context.get("goal_version", "")) != GOAL_VERSION:
			errors.append("GOAL_MISMATCH")
		if Time.get_ticks_msec() > int(context.get("deadline_ms", 0)):
			errors.append("DEADLINE_EXPIRED")
		if str(context.get("policy_hash", "")) != _policy_hash or str(context.get("config_hash", "")) != _config_hash:
			errors.append("CONFIG_IDENTITY_MISMATCH")
	var envelope_ref: Dictionary = envelope.get("state_ref", {})
	if int(envelope_ref.get("revision", -1)) != int(context.get("expected_revision", -2)):
		errors.append("REVISION_MISMATCH")
	if str(envelope_ref.get("sha256", "")) != str(context.get("expected_sha256", "")):
		errors.append("STATE_HASH_MISMATCH")
	var body_value: Variant = envelope.get("body", {})
	if not body_value is Dictionary:
		errors.append("BODY_SCHEMA_INVALID")
		return CanonicalState.sorted_unique(errors)
	var body: Dictionary = body_value as Dictionary
	errors.append_array(_validate_decision_body(body))
	# Structural, hash, session, epoch, request, goal, policy, and deadline
	# binding must all pass before a consumed action can short-circuit freshness.
	if not errors.is_empty():
		return CanonicalState.sorted_unique(errors)
	var steps: Array = body.get("steps", [])
	if not steps.is_empty() and steps[0] is Dictionary:
		var action_id := str((steps[0] as Dictionary).get("action_id", ""))
		if not action_id.is_empty() and _issued_actions.has(action_id):
			return ["DUPLICATE_ACTION"]
	if int(current_state_ref.get("revision", -1)) != int(context.get("expected_revision", -2)):
		errors.append("CURRENT_REVISION_CHANGED")
	if str(current_state_ref.get("sha256", "")) != str(context.get("expected_sha256", "")):
		errors.append("CURRENT_STATE_CHANGED")
	return CanonicalState.sorted_unique(errors)


func _validate_decision_body(body: Dictionary) -> Array:
	var errors: Array = []
	var required := ["intent_id", "typed_intent", "plan_id", "steps", "planning_context_digest", "status", "error"]
	for key in required:
		if not body.has(key):
			errors.append("MISSING_BODY_" + str(key).to_upper())
	for key in body.keys():
		if key not in required:
			errors.append("EXTRA_BODY_" + str(key).to_upper())
	var intent_id := str(body.get("intent_id", ""))
	if not _valid_identifier(intent_id):
		errors.append("INTENT_ID_INVALID")
	var plan_id_value: Variant = body.get("plan_id", null)
	if plan_id_value != null and not _valid_identifier(str(plan_id_value)):
		errors.append("PLAN_ID_INVALID")
	var error_value: Variant = body.get("error", null)
	if error_value != null and (not error_value is String or str(error_value).length() > 256):
		errors.append("ERROR_FIELD_INVALID")
	var typed_value: Variant = body.get("typed_intent", {})
	var typed: Dictionary = {}
	if typed_value is Dictionary:
		typed = typed_value
	else:
		errors.append("TYPED_INTENT_SCHEMA_INVALID")
	if typed.is_empty():
		errors.append("TYPED_INTENT_SCHEMA_INVALID")
	else:
		var typed_required := ["label", "claims", "target"]
		for key in typed_required:
			if not typed.has(key):
				errors.append("MISSING_TYPED_INTENT_" + str(key).to_upper())
		for key in typed.keys():
			if key not in typed_required:
				errors.append("EXTRA_TYPED_INTENT_" + str(key).to_upper())
		if str(typed.get("label", "")) not in ["ASK_INFORMATION", "REQUEST_DISCLOSURE", "REPORT_FACT", "REFUSE_HELP", "AMBIGUOUS", "UNKNOWN"]:
			errors.append("TYPED_INTENT_LABEL_INVALID")
		var target_value: Variant = typed.get("target", null)
		if target_value != null and (not target_value is String or str(target_value).length() > 128):
			errors.append("TYPED_INTENT_TARGET_INVALID")
		var claims_value: Variant = typed.get("claims", [])
		if not claims_value is Array:
			errors.append("CLAIMS_SCHEMA_INVALID")
		else:
			var claims: Array = claims_value
			if claims.size() > 8:
				errors.append("CLAIMS_LIMIT_EXCEEDED")
			for claim in claims:
				if not claim is Dictionary or claim.keys().size() != 2 or not claim.has("code") or not claim.has("state"):
					errors.append("CLAIM_SCHEMA_INVALID")
					continue
				if str(claim.get("code", "")) not in ["LENS_REACHABLE", "LENS_CARRIED", "SIGNAL_RESTORED", "TIDE_LEAD_AUTHORIZED", "GOAL_REACHED", "IN_DIALOGUE", "NONE"]:
					errors.append("CLAIM_CODE_INVALID")
				if str(claim.get("state", "")) not in ["UNVERIFIED", "SUPPORTED", "CONTRADICTED", "INDETERMINATE", "NOT_VISIBLE"]:
					errors.append("CLAIM_STATE_INVALID")
	var digest := str(body.get("planning_context_digest", ""))
	if not _is_hex64(digest):
		errors.append("PLANNING_DIGEST_INVALID")
	var steps_value: Variant = body.get("steps", [])
	if not steps_value is Array:
		errors.append("STEPS_SCHEMA_INVALID")
		return CanonicalState.sorted_unique(errors)
	var steps: Array = steps_value
	if steps.size() > MAX_PLAN_EXPANSIONS:
		errors.append("PLAN_STEPS_LIMIT_EXCEEDED")
	for step in steps:
		if not step is Dictionary:
			errors.append("PLAN_STEP_SCHEMA_INVALID")
			continue
		var step_required := ["action_id", "operation", "execution_mode", "arguments"]
		for key in step_required:
			if not step.has(key):
				errors.append("MISSING_STEP_" + str(key).to_upper())
		for key in step.keys():

			if key not in step_required:
				errors.append("EXTRA_STEP_" + str(key).to_upper())
		var operation := str(step.get("operation", ""))
		var expected_mode: String = str({"acquire_object": "player_request", "install_lens": "player_request", "reveal_hint": "commit_world", "clarify": "dialogue", "negotiate": "dialogue"}.get(operation, ""))
		if expected_mode.is_empty() or str(step.get("execution_mode", "")) != expected_mode:
			errors.append("STEP_BINDING_INVALID")
		var arguments_value: Variant = step.get("arguments", {})
		if not arguments_value is Dictionary:
			errors.append("STEP_ARGUMENTS_SCHEMA_INVALID")
			continue
		var arguments: Dictionary = arguments_value
		var expected_arguments: Dictionary = {}
		match operation:
			"acquire_object", "install_lens":
				expected_arguments = {"object_id": "signal_lens"}
			"reveal_hint":
				expected_arguments = {"actor_id": NPC_ID, "fact_id": "tide_marks_hint"}
			"clarify", "negotiate":
				expected_arguments = {}
		if arguments != expected_arguments:
			errors.append("STEP_ARGUMENTS_INVALID")
		if not _valid_identifier(str(step.get("action_id", ""))):
			errors.append("STEP_ACTION_ID_INVALID")
	return CanonicalState.sorted_unique(errors)

func _valid_identifier(value: String) -> bool:
	if value.is_empty() or value.length() > 128:
		return false
	var regex := RegEx.new()
	regex.compile("^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,127}$")
	return regex.search(value) != null


func _is_hex64(value: String) -> bool:
	if value.length() != 64:
		return false
	for character in value:
		if "0123456789abcdef".find(character) == -1:
			return false
	return true

func _normalize_wire_for_hash(envelope: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var normalized := envelope.duplicate(true)
	for key in ["session_epoch", "sequence", "delivery_index"]:
		if normalized.has(key):
			normalized[key] = int(normalized.get(key, 0))
	var state_ref: Variant = normalized.get("state_ref", null)
	if state_ref is Dictionary and state_ref.has("revision"):
		state_ref["revision"] = int(state_ref.get("revision", 0))
	var budget: Variant = normalized.get("budget", null)
	if budget is Dictionary:
		for key in ["intent_deadline_ms", "episode_deadline_ms", "max_intent_calls", "max_action_attempts", "max_replans", "max_plan_expansions", "max_inflight_per_session"]:
			if budget.has(key):
				budget[key] = int(budget.get(key, 0))
	return normalized

func _validate_common(envelope: Dictionary, expected_type: String) -> Array:
	var errors: Array = []
	var required := [
		"schema_version", "message_type", "session_id", "session_epoch", "episode_id",
		"request_id", "event_id", "sequence", "delivery_index", "scenario_id", "npc_id",
		"goal_id", "goal_version", "policy_hash", "config_hash", "state_ref", "body", "budget", "provenance", "content_sha256",
	]
	for key in required:
		if not envelope.has(key):
			errors.append("MISSING_" + str(key).to_upper())
	for key in envelope.keys():
		if key not in required:
			errors.append("EXTRA_" + str(key).to_upper())
	if str(envelope.get("schema_version", "")) != SCHEMA_VERSION:
		errors.append("SCHEMA_VERSION_MISMATCH")
	if str(envelope.get("message_type", "")) != expected_type:
		errors.append("MESSAGE_TYPE_MISMATCH")
	if str(envelope.get("session_id", "")) != _session_id:
		errors.append("SESSION_MISMATCH")
	if int(envelope.get("session_epoch", -1)) != _session_epoch:
		errors.append("EPOCH_MISMATCH")
	if str(envelope.get("episode_id", "")) != _episode_id:
		errors.append("EPISODE_MISMATCH")
	if str(envelope.get("scenario_id", "")) != SCENARIO_ID:
		errors.append("SCENARIO_MISMATCH")
	if str(envelope.get("npc_id", "")) != NPC_ID:
		errors.append("NPC_MISMATCH")
	if str(envelope.get("goal_id", "")) != GOAL_ID or str(envelope.get("goal_version", "")) != GOAL_VERSION:
		errors.append("GOAL_MISMATCH")
	if str(envelope.get("policy_hash", "")) != _policy_hash:
		errors.append("POLICY_HASH_MISMATCH")
	if str(envelope.get("config_hash", "")) != _config_hash:
		errors.append("CONFIG_HASH_MISMATCH")
	var content := envelope.duplicate(true)
	content.erase("content_sha256")
	if str(envelope.get("content_sha256", "")) != CanonicalState.sha256(_normalize_wire_for_hash(content)):
		errors.append("CONTENT_HASH_MISMATCH")
	return CanonicalState.sorted_unique(errors)


func _sha256_utf8(value: String) -> String:
	var context := HashingContext.new()
	if context.start(HashingContext.HASH_SHA256) != OK:
		return ""
	if context.update(value.to_utf8_buffer()) != OK:
		return ""
	return context.finish().hex_encode()

func _build_envelope(message_type: String, request_id: String, body: Dictionary, state_ref: Dictionary, raw_hash: String) -> Dictionary:
	_sequence += 1
	_delivery_index += 1
	var envelope := {
		"schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
		"message_type": message_type,
		"session_id": _session_id,
		"session_epoch": _session_epoch,
		"episode_id": _episode_id,
		"request_id": request_id,
		"event_id": _new_id("event"),
		"sequence": _sequence,
		"delivery_index": _delivery_index,
		"scenario_id": SCENARIO_ID,
		"npc_id": NPC_ID,
		"goal_id": GOAL_ID,
		"goal_version": GOAL_VERSION,
		"policy_hash": _policy_hash,
		"config_hash": _config_hash,
		"state_ref": {"revision": int(state_ref.get("revision", 0)), "sha256": str(state_ref.get("sha256", ""))},
		"body": body,
		"budget": {
			"intent_deadline_ms": INTENT_DEADLINE_MS,
			"episode_deadline_ms": EPISODE_DEADLINE_MS,
			"max_intent_calls": MAX_INTENT_CALLS,
			"max_action_attempts": MAX_ACTION_ATTEMPTS,
			"max_replans": MAX_REPLANS,
			"max_plan_expansions": MAX_PLAN_EXPANSIONS,
			"max_inflight_per_session": MAX_INFLIGHT,
			"input_tokens": null,
			"output_tokens": null,
			"cost": null,
		},
		"provenance": {
			"arm": "P",
			"backend": "codex",
			"model": null,
			"trace_ref": null,
			"recorded_response_ref": null,
			"raw_utterance_sha256": raw_hash if raw_hash != "" else null,
		},
	}
	envelope["content_sha256"] = CanonicalState.sha256(envelope)
	return envelope


func _send(path: String, envelope: Dictionary) -> bool:
	if _capability.is_empty() or _capability.length() > 512 or _capability.find("\r") != -1 or _capability.find("\n") != -1:
		protocol_error.emit("CAPABILITY_UNAVAILABLE")
		return false
	var payload := JSON.stringify(envelope)
	var headers := PackedStringArray(["Content-Type: application/json", "X-NPC-Capability: Bearer " + _capability])
	var error := _http.request(ENDPOINT + path, headers, HTTPClient.METHOD_POST, payload)
	if error != OK:
		protocol_error.emit("REQUEST_ERROR_%s" % error)
		return false
	return true


func _on_request_completed(result: int, response_code: int, _headers: PackedStringArray, body: PackedByteArray) -> void:
	var context := _pending.duplicate(true)
	_pending.clear()
	if context.is_empty():
		return
	if int(context.get("generation", -1)) != _request_generation or int(context.get("session_epoch", -1)) != _session_epoch:
		return
	if result == HTTPRequest.RESULT_TIMEOUT:
		_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | TIMEOUT")
		protocol_error.emit("TIMEOUT")
		return
	if result != HTTPRequest.RESULT_SUCCESS or response_code < 200 or response_code >= 300:
		_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | UNAVAILABLE")
		var detail := ""
		if not body.is_empty():
			var failure: Variant = JSON.parse_string(body.get_string_from_utf8())
			if failure is Dictionary:
				detail = str(failure.get("error", ""))
				if failure.get("body") is Dictionary:
					detail = str(failure["body"].get("error", detail))
			detail = detail.substr(0, 128).replace("\r", " ").replace("\n", " ")
		protocol_error.emit("HTTP_%s_%s%s" % [result, response_code, "" if detail.is_empty() else ":" + detail])
		return
	var parsed: Variant = JSON.parse_string(body.get_string_from_utf8())
	if not (parsed is Dictionary):
		_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | INVALID RESPONSE")
		protocol_error.emit("INVALID_JSON")
		return
	var envelope: Dictionary = parsed
	var expected := "decision" if str(context.get("kind", "")) == "step" else "ack"
	var errors := _validate_common(envelope, expected)
	if not errors.is_empty():
		_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | PROTOCOL ERROR")
		protocol_error.emit("|".join(errors))
		return
	if str(envelope.get("request_id", "")) != str(context.get("request_id", "")):
		_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | PROTOCOL ERROR")
		protocol_error.emit("REQUEST_MISMATCH")
		return
	if str(context.get("kind", "")) == "step":
		_last_decision_context = context.duplicate(true)
		_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | PLAN RECEIVED")
		decision_received.emit(envelope, context)
	else:
		_set_status("BACKEND CODEX | MODE LIVE | RESULT ACKNOWLEDGED")
		result_recorded.emit(envelope, context)


func _cancel_http_if_pending(_reason: String) -> void:
	if _pending.is_empty() or _http == null:
		return
	_http.cancel_request()
	_pending.clear()


func _sanitize_observation(observation: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	return {
		"lens_reachable": bool(observation.get("lens_reachable", false)),
		"lens_carried": bool(observation.get("lens_carried", false)),
		"signal_restored": bool(observation.get("signal_restored", false)),
		"tide_lead_authorized": bool(observation.get("tide_lead_authorized", false)),
		"goal_reached": bool(observation.get("goal_reached", false)),
		"in_dialogue": bool(observation.get("in_dialogue", false)),
	}


func _new_id(prefix: String) -> String:
	_sequence += 1
	return "%s-%s" % [prefix, CanonicalState.sha256("%s:%s:%s" % [prefix, Time.get_ticks_usec(), _sequence]).substr(0, 24)]


func _set_status(text: String) -> void:
	status_changed.emit(text)
	if _ui != null and is_instance_valid(_ui):
		_ui.set_npc_status(text)

===== END SOURCE FILE =====
